index:
  - heading: "Stars"
    type: table
    include: 
      star[].parent: "First Lessers Star"
    order:
      - by: star[].publications[0].date
      - by: star[].publications[0].order
    columns:
      Star: star[].name
      Published: star[].publications[0].date
      Publisher(s): star[].publications[0].publishers
      Description: star[].desc
---

The Quickening System is one of the largest [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Science/Phenomena/Stars|star systems]] in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Natural Features/Sky|sky]], centered around the host of the system, the First Lessers Star, the system tracks the rapid expansion of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Science/Phenomena/Gods|Gods]] which occurred during the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/God-War Era/Quickening|Quickening]]. The First Lessers Star, created by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter]], documents their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|Elemental Gods]]. Every other star in the system, of which there are a total 428, was published by other Gods to document the creation of their own children via [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Science/Phenomena/Gods#Creation & Domains|domain subdivision]].

The quickening system is located directly above the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Abstract Features/Central Pole|central pole]] and clustered together in a spherical shape. The furthest star from the host is the ???-19 Isotopes Star, which is located 1.4k km from the First Lessers Star. The closest is the New Motion Star, which is only 46.9 km from the host. 
# History
The Quickening System would be created over the course of 58 days and document the creation of every known and unknown God. The host star of the system, the First Lessers Star, would be created on the 12th of Winter [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Indexes/History/Decades/170s BT#178 BT|178 BT]] and documented the creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|Elemental Gods]] by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter]]. It would be followed later in the same day by the creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy|Energy Duo]] by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Energy]], documented in the Energy Division Star. The fifth star in the system would be the last published by one of the Creation Quadrinity, the Dynamic Time Star documenting the creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Past, Present, and Future|Time Trinity]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Time]].

The final star of the Quickening System, the Fifth Generation Vapors Star would be published on the 26th of Spring, [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Indexes/History/Decades/170s BT|177 BT]]. It would be followed shortly after by [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Death's Star System/Death's Star|Death's Star]], ending the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/God-War Era/Quickening|Quickening]] and beginning the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/God-War Era/Dying Period|Dying Period]].
# Famous Translations
The translation of a single star can take years of patience 

It is notoriously difficult to translate an entire star system, especially one as large as the Quickening System. For that reason, many complete translations of the system are compiled using other [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Science/Branches/Astronomy|astronomer's]] translations. Translations of the Quickening System are often compiled for use in [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Science/Models/Godly Lineage Models|Godly Lineage Models]]. The most commonly used model of the system is the Kaleidoscope Model, originally completely translated by [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Temporary Kaleidoscope|Temporary Kaleidoscope]], it was only formally published posthumously in *[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Arts/Useful Arts/Literature/Non-fiction/The Collected Works of Kaleidoscope|The Collected Works of Kaleidoscope]]*.
![[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Science/Models/Kaleidoscope Lineage Model.canvas]]

| First Molecules Star | 59th of Winter, 178 BT | [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Carbon\|God of Carbon]] | The sixth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System\|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Carbon\|God of Carbon]] to document their creation of the first [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Molecular Gods\|Molecular Gods]], the first generation of Carbon Molecule Gods, made together with their Isotopes. |
| Silicon Isotopes Star | 59th of Winter, 178 BT | [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods\|God of Silicon]] | The seventh star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System\|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods\|God of Silicon]] to document their creation of the Silicon [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods\|Isotope Gods]]. |
| Nitrogen Isotopes Star | 59th of Winter, 178 BT | [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods\|God of Nitrogen]] | The eighth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System\|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods\|God of Nitrogen]] to document their creation of the Nitrogen [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods\|Isotope Gods]]. |
| New Energy Division Star | 59th of Winter, 178 BT | [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy\|Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy]] | The ninth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System\|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy\|Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods\|Energy Gods]], who preside over the basal forms of energy. |
| Iron Isotopes Star | 59th of Winter, 178 BT | [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Iron\|God of Iron]] | The tenth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System\|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Iron\|God of Iron]] to document their creation of the Iron [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods\|Isotope Gods]]. |
| First Silicon Molecules Star | 59th of Winter, 178 BT | [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods\|God of Silicon]] | The eleventh star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System\|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods\|God of Silicon]] to document their creation of the first generation of Silicon [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Molecular Gods\|Molecule Gods]], made together with their Isotopes. |

"star_desc": "The host star of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]]. Created by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|Elemental Gods]]."
"publisher1": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter]]"
"pub_desc1": "**[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|The First Lessers Star]]** is published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|Elemental Gods]]. The star motivates other Gods to undergo their own domain subdivision, becoming the host of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]]."
"star_desc2": "Second star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Energy]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy|Energy Duo]], inspired by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter's]] publication of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|First Lessers Star]]."
"publisher2": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Energy]]"
"pub_desc2": "**[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|The Energy Division Star]]** is published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Energy]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy|Energy Duo]]. It becomes the second star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]]."
"pub_desc428": "**[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|The Fifth Generation Vapors Star]]** is published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Material State Gods|Material State Gods]] and God of Sublimation to document the creation of various Vapor Gods. It is the 428th and final star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]]."
"star_desc3": "The third star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Carbon|God of Carbon]] to document their creation of the first [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods|Isotope Gods]], the first generation of Carbon isotopes."
"publisher3": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Carbon|God of Carbon]]"
"star_desc4": "The fourth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|God of Helium]] to document their creation of the Helium [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods|Isotope Gods]]."
"publisher4": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|God of Helium]]"
"star_desc5": "The fifth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Time]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Past, Present, and Future|Time Trinity]], after being coaxed by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|Gods of Matter and Energy]] into subdividing their domain."
"publisher5": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Time]]"
"star_desc6": "The sixth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Carbon|God of Carbon]] to document their creation of the first [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Molecular Gods|Molecular Gods]], the first generation of Carbon Molecule Gods, made together with their Isotopes."
"star_desc7": "The seventh star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|God of Silicon]] to document their creation of the Silicon [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods|Isotope Gods]]."
"publisher7": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|God of Silicon]]"
"star_desc8": "The eighth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|God of Nitrogen]] to document their creation of the Nitrogen [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods|Isotope Gods]]."
"publisher8": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods|God of Nitrogen]]"
"star_desc9": "The ninth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy|Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy]] to document their creation of the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods|Energy Gods]], who preside over the basal forms of energy."
"publisher9": "[[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Energy Gods/Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy|Gods of Kinetic & Potential Energy]]"
"star_desc10": "The tenth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Elemental Gods/God of Iron|God of Iron]] to document their creation of the Iron [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Isotope Gods|Isotope Gods]]."

"star_desc12": "The twelfth star in the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/Geography/Stars/Star Systems/Quickening System|Quickening System]], published by the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Matter]] and the [[Encyclopedia Mysenvaria/History/Biographies/Gods/Gods of Matter, Energy, Time, and Space|God of Energy]] to document their creation of the God of Structure, who presides over the structure of matter."
